**parameters**　[{"parameter": "in_oriented_imagery_dataset", "label_zh": "定向影像資料集", "label_en": "Oriented Imagery Dataset", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Oriented Imagery Layer", "description_en": "The path and name of the oriented imagery dataset with the properties that will be updated."}, {"parameter": "maximum_distance", "label_zh": "最大距離 （可選）", "label_en": "Maximum Distance (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Double", "description_en": "The maximum search distance used when querying the dataset features. The maximum distance cannot be less than zero. The distance is measured in meters."}, {"parameter": "coverage_percent", "label_zh": "覆蓋范圍百分比 （可選）", "label_en": "Coverage Percentage (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Double", "description_en": "A percentage that modifies the extent of the image's ground footprint. The ground footprint of each image is computed to search for images that contain the selected location, which is identified as a red cross on the map. A negative percentage value reduces the size of the ground footprint and a positive percentage value increases it. This parameter can be used to exclude or include points on the edge of an image. For example, a value of -30 will reduce the size of the footprint by 30 percent and a value of 20 will increase it by 20 percent. Valid values range from -50 to 50."}, {"parameter": "footprint_item", "label_zh": "覆蓋區項目 （可選）", "label_en": "Footprint Item (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The name of the footprint feature class. The feature class should be in the same geodatabase as the oriented imagery dataset."}, {"parameter": "elevation_source", "label_zh": "高程源 （可選）", "label_en": "Elevation Source (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the elevation source that will be used. DEM—The elevation source will be a digital elevation model that is a dynamic image service or a tile image service. Constant Elevation—The elevation source will be a constant ground elevation value for the entire dataset. None—The elevation source will be removed."}, {"parameter": "constant_elevation", "label_zh": "常量高程 （可選）", "label_en": "Constant Elevation (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Double", "description_en": "The constant ground elevation value for the entire dataset. The Vertical Measurement Unit parameter value will be used as the unit for constant elevation. This parameter is active when the Elevation Source parameter value is Constant Elevation."}, {"parameter": "dem", "label_zh": "數字高程模型 （可選）", "label_en": "Digital Elevation Model (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Image Service", "description_en": "The URL that references the input digital elevation model. A dynamic image service or a tile image service can be used as the digital elevation model. This parameter is active when the Elevation Source parameter value is DEM."}, {"parameter": "lod", "label_zh": "細節層次 （可選）", "label_en": "Level of Detail (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The scale defined in a tiling schema. The scale represents the zoom level value. Each successive level improves resolution and map scale by double when compared to the previous level. This parameter is active when the Digital Elevation Model parameter value is a tile image service."}, {"parameter": "raster_function", "label_zh": "網格函數 （可選）", "label_en": "Raster Function (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The raster function processing template that will be applied to the image service. This parameter is active when the Digital Elevation Model parameter value is a dynamic image service."}, {"parameter": "vertical_measurement_unit", "label_zh": "垂直測量單位 （可選）", "label_en": "Vertical Measurement Unit (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the unit that will be used for all vertical measurements. Meter—Meters will be used as the unit of measurement. Feet—Feet will be used as the unit of measurement."}, {"parameter": "time_interval_unit", "label_zh": "時間間隔單位 （可選）", "label_en": "Time Interval Unit (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the time measurement unit that will be used to filter images. Minutes—Images will be filtered by minutes. Hours—Images will be filtered by hours. Days—Images will be filtered by days. Weeks—Images will be filtered by weeks. Months—Images will be filtered by months. Years—Images will be filtered by years."}, {"parameter": "oriented_imagery_type", "label_zh": "定向影像類型 （可選）", "label_en": "Oriented Imagery Type (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the type of images in the dataset. Horizontal—Images in which the exposure is parallel to the ground and looking to the horizon are in the dataset. Oblique—Images in which the exposure is at an angle to the ground, typically at approximately 45 degrees, so the sides of objects can be seen are in the dataset. Nadir—Images in which the exposure is perpendicular to the ground and looking straight down are in the dataset. Only the top of objects can be seen. 360—Images taken using cameras that provide 360-degree spherical surround views or have been stitched together as 360-degree views from multiple cameras are in the dataset. Inspection—Close-up images of assets are in the dataset. AerialFrameVideo—Video captured by a drone or aircraft with the camera pointing downward towards the ground. TerrestrialFrameVideo—Video captured using handheld devices such as dashboard cameras. Terrestrial360Video—A 360-degree video captured using devices such as GoPro or Insta360. None—The oriented imagery type will be removed from the dataset."}, {"parameter": "camera_heading", "label_zh": "相機朝向 （可選）", "label_en": "Camera Heading (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Double", "description_en": "The camera orientation of the first rotation around the z-axis of the camera. The value is in degrees. The heading values are measured in the positive clockwise direction in which north is defined as 0 degrees. -999 is used when the orientation is unknown."}, {"parameter": "camera_pitch", "label_zh": "相機仰俯角 （可選）", "label_en": "Camera Pitch (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Double", "description_en": "The camera orientation of the second rotation around the x-axis of the camera in the positive counterclockwise direction. The value is in degrees. The pitch is 0 degrees when the camera is facing straight down to the ground. The valid range of pitch is from 0 to 180 degrees, with 180 degrees for a camera facing straight up and 90 degrees for a camera facing the horizon."}, {"parameter": "camera_roll", "label_zh": "相機轉動 （可選）", "label_en": "Camera Roll (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Double", "description_en": "The camera orientation of the final rotation around the z-axis of the camera in the positive clockwise direction. The value is in degrees. Valid values range from -90 to 90."}, {"parameter": "camera_height", "label_zh": "相機高度 （可選）", "label_en": "Camera Height (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Double", "description_en": "The height of the camera above the ground when the imagery was captured. The unit is meters. Camera height is used to determine the visible extent of the image. Large values will result in a greater view extent. Values should not be less than 0."}, {"parameter": "hfov", "label_zh": "水平視域 （可選）", "label_en": "Horizontal Field of View (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Double", "description_en": "The camera's scope in the horizontal direction. The value is in degrees. Valid values range from 0 to 360."}, {"parameter": "vfov", "label_zh": "垂直視域 （可選）", "label_en": "Vertical Field of View (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Double", "description_en": "The camera's scope in the vertical direction. The value is in degrees. Valid values range from 0 to 180."}, {"parameter": "near_distance", "label_zh": "近距離 （可選）", "label_en": "Near Distance (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Double", "description_en": "The nearest usable distance of the imagery from the camera position. The unit is meters."}, {"parameter": "far_distance", "label_zh": "遠距離 （可選）", "label_en": "Far Distance (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Double", "description_en": "The farthest usable distance of the imagery from the camera position. The unit is meters. Far distances should be greater than 0."}, {"parameter": "image_rotation", "label_zh": "圖像旋轉 （可選）", "label_en": "Image Rotation (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Double", "description_en": "The orientation of the camera in degrees relative to the scene when the image was captured. The rotation is in addition to the camera roll. Valid values range from-360 to 360."}, {"parameter": "orientation_accuracy", "label_zh": "定向精度 （可選）", "label_en": "Orientation Accuracy (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The orientation accuracy values. The values are represented as a string of eight semicolon-delimited values that define the standard deviation of each value in the following order: Camera location in XY direction Camera Z Camera Heading Camera Pitch Camera Roll Near distance Far distance Elevation of the terrain All distances are defined in meters. All angles are defined in degrees. If a value is unknown, that value can be excluded. However, this may result in the accuracy computed being higher than the actual value. For example, if a GPS has a +/- 10 meters root mean square (RMS) error in x,y-coordinates and +/- 20 meters in height, the orientation accuracy value is 10;20. The typical value for a vehicle-mounted consumer camera is 15;10;5;5;5;0;0;1. The typical value for a 360 camera with autoleveling (for example, GoPro or Insta360) is 15;0.2;0.5;0.5;0.5;0;0;1. The typical value for a Drone imagery without Real Time Kinematic (RTK) and standard gimble is 5;10;2;2;2;0;0;1. The typical value for oblique imagery following aerial triangulation is 0.01;0.02;0.001;0.001;0.001;0;0;1. The values provided above are intended for reference only and should not be treated as absolute measurements. To ensure precise orientation accuracy parameters, the values should be taken directly from the system that captured the imagery. For aerial imagery, these parameters are typically available in the Aerial Triangulation results, if that process has been completed. For systems that record GPS‑based positional information, the GPS accuracy details should be supplied as part of the dataset."}, {"parameter": "image_path_prefix", "label_zh": "圖像路徑前綴 （可選）", "label_en": "Image Path Prefix (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The prefix that will be used to build the image path in conjunction with the ImagePath attribute."}, {"parameter": "image_path_suffix", "label_zh": "圖像路徑后綴 （可選）", "label_en": "Image Path Suffix (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The suffix that will be used to build the image path in conjunction with the ImagePath attribute."}, {"parameter": "depth_image_path_prefix", "label_zh": "深度圖像路徑前綴 （可選）", "label_en": "Depth Image Path Prefix (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The prefix that will be used to build the depth image path in conjunction with the Depth Image attribute."}, {"parameter": "depth_image_path_suffix", "label_zh": "深度圖像路徑后綴 （可選）", "label_en": "Depth Image Path Suffix (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The suffix that will be used to build the depth image path in conjunction with the Depth Image attribute."}, {"parameter": "dem_path_prefix", "label_zh": "DEM 路徑前綴 （可選）", "label_en": "DEM Path Prefix (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The prefix that will be used to build the digital elevation model path in conjunction with the Elevation Source URL in the attribute."}, {"parameter": "dem_path_suffix", "label_zh": "DEM 路徑后綴 （可選）", "label_en": "DEM Path Suffix (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The suffix that will be used to build the digital elevation model path in conjunction with the Elevation Source URL in the attribute."}, {"parameter": "image_geometry_field", "label_zh": "圖像幾何欄位 （可選）", "label_en": "Image Geometry Field (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The field for a feature layer that will be overlayed on the image in the oriented imagery viewer. This field will store the image space geometry of the feature to be overlayed. The default is ImageGeometry."}, {"parameter": "image_reference_field", "label_zh": "圖像參考欄位 （可選）", "label_en": "Image Reference Field (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The field for a feature layer that will be overlayed on the image in the oriented imagery viewer. The field will store the unique reference ID of the image in which the feature to be overlayed was created. The default is OIObjectID."}, {"parameter": "reference_id_field", "label_zh": "參考 ID 欄位 （可選）", "label_en": "Reference ID Field (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "This field is part of a feature class that includes features captured from the oriented imagery dataset. The field will store the unique reference ID corresponding to the specific image on which each feature was recorded. The default is ObjectID."}, {"parameter": "sequence_order_field", "label_zh": "順序編號欄位 （可選）", "label_en": "Sequence Order Field (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Legacy: This parameter has been deprecated."}, {"parameter": "vertical_search_range", "label_zh": "垂直搜索范圍 （可選）", "label_en": "Vertical Search Range (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Double", "description_en": "The maximum vertical search distance used when querying dataset features. This value must be greater than or equal to zero. The unit of measurement is meters."}, {"parameter": "image_access_connections", "label_zh": "", "label_en": "Image Access Connections (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "File; Folder", "description_en": "The ArcGIS Cloud Storage Connection file (.acs)."}, {"parameter": "sequence_order_fields", "label_zh": "", "label_en": "Sequence Order Fields (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Value Table", "description_en": "This field stores a unique sequence of numbers that controls the navigation and sorting order through features. The values of these fields are used to reorder the sequence of image navigation. The fields will be processed in the order they are specified, from first to last. The default field is SequenceOrder. By default, the sorting order is ascending. Value table columns: Field(s)—Specifies the name of field(s). Order—Specifies the sorting order of the field(s)."}]

**text**　雖然此錯誤可能會發生，但發生的頻率非常低，以至于我們尚且無法確定典型原因，也因此目前沒有可用的解決方案。
如果您能夠使用反饋表單來提供有關導致此錯誤的所用步驟的詳細信息，那么此舉將有助于我們在未來確定和提供解決方案。
幫助信息包括下列內容：
消息 ID 以及錯誤或警告消息
工具的名稱
用于參數的值
相關資料集詳細信息
如果該工具不再處于打開狀態，則您可以在地理處理歷史記錄中找到這些詳細信息。
我們對您所提供的一切信息深表感激。 感謝您為我們改進錯誤代碼文檔所做出的貢獻。

## 更新網絡分析圖層(網絡分析分析工具) · 摘要摘要

**id**　00b0c5c19940b3cecf-1

**english**　Upgrade Network Analysis Layer

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 資料處理工具
- 網絡分析員工具箱
- 分析工具et

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/network-analyst/upgrade-network-analysis-layer.html#summary

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/network-analyst/upgrade-network-analysis-layer.html

**text**　升級在早期發行的ArcGISPro中創建的網絡分析層, 以包含當前發布支持的所有功能。 一些解決方案解答者網絡分析層的圖案在某些 ArcGIS Pro 發布時更新, 以滿足不同工作流程的要求。 當您創建一個新的網絡分析層時, 它使用當前發布軟件的圖案。 但是, 在較早發布版本中創建的現有網絡分析層并不自動更新, 與當前發布版本的圖案匹配。 此工具將缺失的子層和欄位添加到輸入網絡分析層, 允許您訪問此額外功能 。 更多了解網絡分析層系統更新及其釋放過程

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "Upgrades a network analysis layer created in an earlier release of ArcGIS Pro to include all functionality supported by the current release.\nThe schema of network analysis layers for some solvers was updated at certain ArcGIS Pro releases to accommodate the requirements of different workflows. When you create a new network analysis layer, it uses the schema of the current software release. However, existing network analysis layers created in an earlier software release are not automatically updated to match the current release's schema. This tool adds missing sublayers and fields to the input network analysis layer, allowing you to access this additional functionality.\nLearn more about network analysis layer schema updates and the releases in which they occurred", "section": "Summary"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/network-analyst/upgrade-network-analysis-layer.html#summary

**text**　import arcpy

# Retrieve the existing network analysis layer named "Last Mile Delivery" from the map
project = arcpy.mp.ArcGISProject("CURRENT")
map_object = project.listMaps()[0]
lmd_layer = map_object.listLayers("Last Mile Delivery")[0]

# Upgrade the layer to a the latest schema
arcpy.na.UpgradeNetworkAnalysisLayer(lmd_layer)

## 更新網絡分析圖層(網絡分析分析工具) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 資料處理工具
- 網絡分析員工具箱
- 分析工具et

**english**　Upgrade Network Analysis Layer

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/network-analyst/upgrade-network-analysis-layer.html

**id**　00b0c5c19940b3cecf-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/network-analyst/upgrade-network-analysis-layer.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Upgrade Network Analysis Layer，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。輸入網絡分析圖層 [in_network_analysis_layer]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/network-analyst/upgrade-network-analysis-layer.html#parameters

## 003713: 用于創建緩存切片的一個或多個并行處理實例失敗。 · 描述

**id**　00b20e2b429c95eb7b-1

**english**　003713: One or more of the parallel processing instances that was creating the cache tiles has failed.

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 工具錯誤和警告
- 3701-3800

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/001001-010000/tool-errors-and-warnings-03701-03725-003713.html#description

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/001001-010000/tool-errors-and-warnings-03701-03725-003713.html

**text**　一個或多個進程在切片創建過程中失敗。 生成的任何包都可能不完整。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/001001-010000/tool-errors-and-warnings-03701-03725-003713.html#description

## 003713: 用于創建緩存切片的一個或多個并行處理實例失敗。 · 解決方案

**id**　00b20e2b429c95eb7b-2

**english**　003713: One or more of the parallel processing instances that was creating the cache tiles has failed.

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 工具錯誤和警告
- 3701-3800

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/001001-010000/tool-errors-and-warnings-03701-03725-003713.html#solution

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/001001-010000/tool-errors-and-warnings-03701-03725-003713.html

**text**　由于各種原因，緩存生成可能失敗。 要解決此錯誤，可以使用以下解決方案之一。
確保計算機內存資源充足，足以成功生成緩存。 標注、制圖或網格組件可能需要額外的 RAM 才能渲染地圖內容。 將并行處理因子環境降低為 50% 有助于降低資源利用率。
正在寫入輸出的磁盤可能沒有足夠的磁盤空間。 要打包切片內容，需要在 zip 存檔文件中創建切片內容副本。 在磁盤空間至少為切片內容所需磁盤空間兩倍的磁盤中選擇輸出文件夾。
確保 ArcGIS Pro 進程可以在生成緩存期間訪問資料，并且所需字體可用。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/001001-010000/tool-errors-and-warnings-03701-03725-003713.html#solution

## 110123: 輸出時空立方體的距離間隔可能與模板立方體稍有不同。 · 描述

**id**　00b36fa8cb1c9a39e5-1

**english**　110123: The Output Space Time Cube may have a Distance Interval that varies slightly from the Template Cube.

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 工具錯誤和警告
- 110101-110200

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/110001-120000/tool-errors-and-warnings-110101-110125-110123.html#description

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/110001-120000/tool-errors-and-warnings-110101-110125-110123.html

**text**　創建 模板立方體 值所用的 距離間隔 參數值與選定的 輸出時空立方體 值之間可能存在細微差別。
如果您在早于 ArcGIS Pro 2.1 的版本中創建了 模板立方體 ，并使用了以英尺為單位的 距離間隔 參數值，則使用的英尺單位為國際英尺。 在更新后的 ArcGIS Pro 2.1 版本中，此設置已更改為使用美國英尺作為單位。 這會導致使用國際英尺單位創建的 模板立方體 值和使用美國英尺單位創建的 輸出時空立方體 值之間的格網像元大小存在細微差別。
但是，如果 模板立方體 參數值的空間參考使用了美國英尺單位，則不會產生上述差別。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/110001-120000/tool-errors-and-warnings-110101-110125-110123.html#description

## WFS 轉圖徵類 (轉換工具) · 用法

**id**　00b3b4b892d9d8966c-2

**english**　WFS To Feature Class

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 轉換工具箱
- 由 WFS 轉出工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/conversion/wfs-to-feature-class.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/conversion/wfs-to-feature-class.html

**text**　從簡單或復雜的 WFS 服務下載圖徵。
當為 WFS 服務器參數指定 URL 時，服務器發布的所有圖徵類型都將列在選擇要提取的圖徵類型參數的選項中。 學校、道路及宗地的 WFS 圖徵類型就是這種圖徵類型。
默認情況下，來自 WFS 源的所有圖徵都會被添加至圖徵類。 可通過輸出范圍 環境設置將圖徵限定為與用戶定義范圍交叉的圖徵。 也可通過環境設置的地理資料庫設置部分來指定輸出配置關鍵字。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/conversion/wfs-to-feature-class.html#usage

**parameter**　out_path

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標注
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 輸出位置
- 輸出圖徵類或地理資料庫的位置。 如果輸入是簡單的 WFS 服務，則輸出位置可以是地理資料庫或地理資料庫內的圖徵資料集。 若輸出位置為圖徵資料集，則座標系將從源座標系轉換為圖徵資料集座標系。 如果輸入是復雜的 WFS 服務，則輸出位置必須是文件夾。
- Workspace; Feature Dataset; Folder

**id**　00b3b4b892d9d8966c-11

**english**　WFS To Feature Class

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 轉換工具箱
- 由 WFS 轉出工具集

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/conversion/wfs-to-feature-class.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/conversion/wfs-to-feature-class.html

**parameter**　swap_xy

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標注
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 交換 XY 軸順序 （可選）
- 指定是否將交換輸出圖徵類的 x,y 軸順序。 某些 WFS 服務可能在服務器端交換了 x,y 座標的順序，從而導致圖徵類顯示不正確。 選中—將交換 x,y 軸順序。 未選中—將不會交換 x,y 軸順序。 這是默認設置。
- Boolean

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/conversion/wfs-to-feature-class.html#parameters

## WFS 轉圖徵類 (轉換工具) · 參數

**id**　00b3b4b892d9d8966c-12

**english**　WFS To Feature Class

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 轉換工具箱
- 由 WFS 轉出工具集

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/conversion/wfs-to-feature-class.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/conversion/wfs-to-feature-class.html

**parameter**　page_size

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標注
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 頁面大小 （可選）
- 從 WFS 服務下載圖徵時將使用的頁面大小。 默認值為 100。 某些服務器會限制單次請求的圖徵數量，或者如果單次請求中包含大量圖徵，服務器性能會下降。 使用此參數可在多個頁面中請求較少數量的圖徵，以避免服務器超時或超出最多圖徵限值。 此參數僅適用于支持 startIndex 和 count WFS 參數的簡單 WFS 2.0 服務。 對于舊版本的 WFS（1.1.0 和 1.0.0），此參數將被忽略。
- Long

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/conversion/wfs-to-feature-class.html#parameters

- 指定是否將交換輸出圖徵類的 x,y 軸順序。 某些 WFS 服務可能在服務器端交換了 x,y 座標的順序，從而導致圖徵類顯示不正確。 SWAP_XY—將交換 x,y 軸順序。 DO_NOT_SWAP_XY—將不會交換 x,y 軸順序。 這是默認設置。

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/conversion/wfs-to-feature-class.html#derived-output

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/conversion/wfs-to-feature-class.html

**parameter**　page_size

**interface**　python

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- page_size （可選）
- 從 WFS 服務下載圖徵時將使用的頁面大小。 默認值為 100。 某些服務器會限制單次請求的圖徵數量，或者如果單次請求中包含大量圖徵，服務器性能會下降。 使用此參數可在多個頁面中請求較少數量的圖徵，以避免服務器超時或超出最多圖徵限值。 此參數僅適用于支持 startIndex 和 count WFS 參數的簡單 WFS 2.0 服務。 對于舊版本的 WFS（1.1.0 和 1.0.0），此參數將被忽略。
- Long

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/conversion/wfs-to-feature-class.html#derived-output

## WFS 轉圖徵類 (轉換工具) · 代碼示例

**id**　00b3b4b892d9d8966c-28

**english**　WFS To Feature Class

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 轉換工具箱
- 由 WFS 轉出工具集

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/conversion/wfs-to-feature-class.html#derived-output

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/conversion/wfs-to-feature-class.html

**text**　# Name: WFSToFeatureClass_example1.py
# Description: Create a feature class from a WFS service

# Import arcpy module
import arcpy

# Set local variables
wfs_service = "http://sampleserver6.arcgisonline.com/arcgis/services/SampleWorldCities/MapServer/WFSServer?request=GetCapabilities&service=WFS"
wfs_featuretype = "cities"
out_location = "C:/Data/Default.gdb"
out_name = "SampleWorldCities"

# Run the WFSToFeatureClass tool
arcpy.conversion.WFSToFeatureClass(wfs_service, wfs_featuretype, out_location, out_name)

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/conversion/wfs-to-feature-class.html#derived-output

## WFS 轉圖徵類 (轉換工具) · 代碼示例

**id**　00b3b4b892d9d8966c-30

**english**　WFS To Feature Class

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 轉換工具箱
- 由 WFS 轉出工具集

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/conversion/wfs-to-feature-class.html#derived-output

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/conversion/wfs-to-feature-class.html

**text**　# Name: WFSToFeatureClass_example2.py
# Description: Create a feature class from a WFS service and use extent environment

# Import arcpy module
import arcpy

# Use arcpy.EnvManager to set the extent to be used by the tool
with arcpy.EnvManager(extent=arcpy.Extent(9.3385, 45.7384250210615, 17.2915, 49.7115749789385, spatial_reference=arcpy.SpatialReference('GCS_WGS_1984'))):
    arcpy.conversion.WFSToFeatureClass(
        input_WFS_server="http://sampleserver6.arcgisonline.com/arcgis/services/SampleWorldCities/MapServer/WFSServer",
        WFS_feature_type="Cities",
        out_path=r"C:\MyProject\MyData.gdb",
        out_name="cities_use_extent",
        is_complex="NOT_COMPLEX",
        max_features=1000,
        expose_metadata="DO_NOT_EXPOSE",
        swap_xy="DO_NOT_SWAP_XY",
        page_size=100
    )

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/conversion/wfs-to-feature-class.html#derived-output

## WFS 轉圖徵類 (轉換工具) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 轉換工具箱
- 由 WFS 轉出工具集

**english**　WFS To Feature Class

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/conversion/wfs-to-feature-class.html

**id**　00b3b4b892d9d8966c-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/conversion/wfs-to-feature-class.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 WFS To Feature Class，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。WFS 服務器 [input_WFS_server]、選擇要提取的圖徵類型 [WFS_feature_type]、輸出位置 [out_path]、名稱 [out_name]、復雜的 WFS 服務 （可選） [is_complex]、最大圖徵數 （可選） [max_features]、顯示元資料 （可選） [expose_metadata]、交換 XY 軸順序 （可選） [swap_xy]、頁面大小 （可選） [page_size]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/conversion/wfs-to-feature-class.html#parameters

**parameters**　[{"parameter": "input_WFS_server", "label_zh": "WFS 服務器", "label_en": "WFS Server", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The URL of the source WFS service (for example, http://sampleserver6.arcgisonline.com/arcgis/services/SampleWorldCities/MapServer/WFSServer?). If the input is a complex WFS service (the Complex WFS service parameter is checked), this can also be the path to the .xml file."}, {"parameter": "WFS_feature_type", "label_zh": "選擇要提取的圖徵類型", "label_en": "Select Feature Type to Extract", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The name of the WFS layer that will be extracted from the input WFS service."}, {"parameter": "out_path", "label_zh": "輸出位置", "label_en": "Output Location", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Workspace; Feature Dataset; Folder", "description_en": "The location of the output feature class or geodatabase. If the input is a simple WFS service, the output location can be a geodatabase or a feature dataset in a geodatabase. If the output location is a feature dataset, the coordinates will be converted from the source coordinate system to the coordinate system of the feature dataset. If the input is a complex WFS service, the output location must be a folder."}, {"parameter": "out_name", "label_zh": "名稱", "label_en": "Name", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The name of the output feature class or geodatabase. If the input is a simple WFS service, the name will be used to create a feature class in the output location. If the feature class name already exists in the geodatabase, the name will be automatically incremented. By default, the feature type name is used. If the input is a complex WFS service, the name will be used to create a geodatabase in the output location."}, {"parameter": "is_complex", "label_zh": "復雜的 WFS 服務 （可選）", "label_en": "Complex WFS service (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether the WFS Server parameter value is a complex WFS service. Checked—The WFS service is a complex WFS service. Unchecked—The WFS service is not a complex WFS service. This is the default."}, {"parameter": "max_features", "label_zh": "最大圖徵數 （可選）", "label_en": "Max Features (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Long", "description_en": "The maximum number of features that can be returned. The default is 1000."}, {"parameter": "expose_metadata", "label_zh": "顯示元資料 （可選）", "label_en": "Expose Metadata (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether metadata tables will be created from the service. This is only applicable to complex WFS services. Checked—Metadata tables will be created in the output geodatabase. Unchecked—Metadata tables will not be created in the output geodatabase. This is the default."}, {"parameter": "swap_xy", "label_zh": "交換 XY 軸順序 （可選）", "label_en": "Swap XY Axis Order (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether the x,y axis order of the output feature class will be swapped. Some WFS services may have the order of the x,y coordinates swapped on the server side, causing the feature class to display incorrectly. Checked—The x,y axis order will be swapped. Unchecked—The x,y axis order will not be swapped. This is the default."}, {"parameter": "page_size", "label_zh": "頁面大小 （可選）", "label_en": "Page Size (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Long", "description_en": "The page size that will be used when downloading features from the WFS service. The default is 100. Some servers limit the number of features that can be requested at a time or server performance may be slow when requesting a large number of features in a single request. Use this parameter to request a smaller number of features in multiple pages to avoid server timeouts or maximum feature limits. This parameter is only applicable for simple WFS 2.0 services that support the startIndex and count WFS parameters. The parameter will be ignored for older versions of WFS (1.1.0 and 1.0.0)."}]

**signatures**　
- arcpy.conversion.WFSToFeatureClass(input_WFS_server, WFS_feature_type, out_path, out_name, {is_complex}, {max_features}, {expose_metadata}, {swap_xy}, {page_size})

**text**　依英文版參數表順序列出：input_WFS_server、WFS_feature_type、out_path、out_name、is_complex、max_features、expose_metadata、swap_xy、page_size。選填標示、資料型別與英文原說明見 parameters。

## 計算欄位 (GeoAnalytics Desktop 工具) · 用法

**id**　00e09879a4e67e6634-2

**english**　Calculate Field

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- GeoAnalytics Desktop 工具箱
- 管理資料工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/geoanalytics-desktop/calculate-field.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/geoanalytics-desktop/calculate-field.html

**text**　計算欄位可運行于表格、點、線或面圖徵上。
計算欄位將始終創建圖層。 該工具不會修改輸入。 您一次只能計算一個欄位的值。
您可以為現有欄位或您通過創建唯一欄位名稱添加的新欄位來計算值。
可以使用 Arcade 表達式創建表達式。 有關詳細信息，請參閱 GeoAnalytics Desktop 工具中的 Arcade 表達式。
Arcade 表達式可為跟蹤感知型。 追蹤感知型表達式要求資料已啟用時間且時間類型為時刻，并且指定追蹤欄位。 要了解有關構建跟蹤感知型表達式的詳細信息，請參閱追蹤感知示例。
要包括追蹤感知型計算，您必須執行以下操作： 使用已啟用時間且時間類型為時刻的圖層。 選中追蹤感知型復選框。 選擇用于標識軌跡的欄位。
追蹤由一個或多個追蹤欄位的唯一組合表示。 例如，如果將 flightID 和 Destination 欄位用作軌跡標識符，則圖徵 ID007、Solden 和 ID007、Tokyo 將分別處于兩個獨立的軌跡，原因是其 Destination 欄位值不同。
還可使用以下方法完成相似性分析： “資料管理”工具箱中的計算欄位 工具
以定義間隔應用時間間隔邊界線段軌跡。 例如，如果您將時間間隔邊界設置為 1 天，開始于 1990 年 1 月 1 日上午 9:00，則軌跡會在每天上午 9:00 被截斷，并就該線段進行分析。 此分割將加快計算時間，因為它會創建較小的軌跡以進行分析。 如果分析更適合按重復的時間間隔邊界進行分割，則建議使用大資料處理。 分析必須為追蹤感知型，才能設置時間界限。
除計算的欄位外，輸出可能包括以下新欄位類型： ObjectID datetime—如果結果已啟用時間。 新欄位 instant_date 的時間類型為時刻，而 start_date 和 end_date 的時間類型為間隔。
您可以執行以下一項或多項操作來提高計算欄位工具的性能。 設置范圍環境，以便僅分析感興趣的資料。 如果您正在使用軌跡，請使用時間界限分割參數來分割軌跡。 如果表達式中包含追蹤表達式，請僅選擇追蹤感知型。 將本地資料用于分析運行的位置。
此地理處理工具由 Spark 支持。 分析在您的臺式計算機上使用多核并行完成。 有關運行分析的詳細信息，請參閱 GeoAnalytics Desktop 工具的注意事項。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/geoanalytics-desktop/calculate-field.html#usage

## 計算欄位 (GeoAnalytics Desktop 工具) · 代碼示例

**id**　00e09879a4e67e6634-29

**english**　Calculate Field

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- GeoAnalytics Desktop 工具箱
- 管理資料工具集

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/geoanalytics-desktop/calculate-field.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/geoanalytics-desktop/calculate-field.html

**text**　# Name: CalculateField.py
# Description: Calculate the mean of the 3 most recent speed measurements in hurricane tracks

# Import system modules
import arcpy

arcpy.env.workspace = "C:/data/Weather.gdb"

# Set local variables
inFeatures = "Hurricanes"
fieldName = "MeanSpeed3"
out = "HurricaneTracks_Mean"
calcExpression = "Date($feature.DateAsString)"

# Run Calculate Field
arcpy.gapro.CalculateField(inFeatures, out, "NEW_FIELD", fieldName, "", "Date",
                           calcExpression)

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/geoanalytics-desktop/calculate-field.html#parameters

## 計算欄位 (GeoAnalytics Desktop 工具) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- GeoAnalytics Desktop 工具箱
- 管理資料工具集

**english**　Calculate Field

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/geoanalytics-desktop/calculate-field.html

**id**　00e09879a4e67e6634-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/geoanalytics-desktop/calculate-field.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Calculate Field，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。輸入圖層 [input_layer]、輸出資料集 [output]、要計算的欄位 [field_to_calculate]、新欄位名稱 （可選） [field_name]、現有欄位 （可選） [existing_field]、欄位類型 （可選） [field_type]、表達式 [expression]、追蹤感知型 （可選） [track_aware]、軌跡欄位 [track_fields]、時間界限分割 （可選） [time_boundary_split]、時間界限參考 （可選） [time_boundary_reference]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/geoanalytics-desktop/calculate-field.html#parameters

Calculated values are applied in the units of the spatial reference of the input unless you are using a geographic coordinate system, in which case they will be in meters. If the layer is added to the map, the Fields and Helpers filters can be used to build an expression."}, {"parameter": "track_aware", "label_zh": "追蹤感知型 （可選）", "label_en": "Track Aware (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether the expression will use a track-aware expression. Checked—The expression will use a track-aware expression, and a track field must be specified. Unchecked—The expression will not use a track-aware expression. This is the default."}, {"parameter": "track_fields", "label_zh": "軌跡欄位", "label_en": "Track Fields (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Field", "description_en": "One or more fields that will be used to identify unique tracks."}, {"parameter": "time_boundary_split", "label_zh": "時間界限分割 （可選）", "label_en": "Time Boundary Split (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Time Unit", "description_en": "A time span to split the input data into for analysis. A time boundary allows you to analyze values within a defined time span. For example, if you use a time boundary of 1 day, and set the time boundary reference to January 1, 1980, tracks will be split at the beginning of every day."}, {"parameter": "time_boundary_reference", "label_zh": "時間界限參考 （可選）", "label_en": "Time Boundary Reference (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Date", "description_en": "The reference time used to split the input data into for analysis. Time boundaries will be created for the entire span of the data, and the reference time does not need to occur at the start. If no reference time is specified, January 1, 1970, is used."}]

**signatures**　
- arcpy.gapro.CalculateField(input_layer, output, field_to_calculate, {field_name}, {existing_field}, {field_type}, expression, {track_aware}, {track_fields}, {time_boundary_split}, {time_boundary_reference})

**text**　依英文版參數表順序列出：input_layer、output、field_to_calculate、field_name、existing_field、field_type、expression、track_aware、track_fields、time_boundary_split、time_boundary_reference。選填標示、資料型別與英文原說明見 parameters。

## 001958：未找到有效的資產組。 · 描述

**id**　00e628bfcc71162195-1

**english**　001958: No valid asset group was found.

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 工具錯誤和警告

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/001001-010000/tool-errors-and-warnings-01951-01975-001958.html#description

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/001001-010000/tool-errors-and-warnings-01951-01975-001958.html

**text**　在執行公共設施網絡的配置任務時，資料集沒有可使用的有效資產組值。
許多配置任務依賴使用資產組和資產類型屬性欄位構建到圖徵類方案中的分類系統。 資產組欄位要求創建有效的子類型值，用于表示資產的主要分類。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/001001-010000/tool-errors-and-warnings-01951-01975-001958.html#description

## 001958：未找到有效的資產組。 · 解決方案

**id**　00e628bfcc71162195-2

**english**　001958: No valid asset group was found.

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 工具錯誤和警告
- 1901-2000

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/001001-010000/tool-errors-and-warnings-01951-01975-001958.html#solution

**text**　檢查網絡屬性以確保資料集具有有效的資產組值。
確保為資料集創建的子類型值有效。 這些是資產組欄位的代表，并作為圖徵類資產的主要分類。
有關詳細信息，請參閱配置公共設施網絡以及在子類型視圖中查看并編輯子類型。

## 了解坡度 · 了解坡度

**id**　00eaafdff66f3961ac-1

**english**　Understanding slope

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 3D Analyst 工具箱
- 表面三角化工具集
- TIN 表面分析概念

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/3d-analyst/understanding-slope.html

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/3d-analyst/understanding-slope.html

**text**　需具備 3D Analyst 許可方可使用。
坡度可表明表面上某個位置的最陡下坡傾斜程度。可針對 TIN 中的每個三角形和網格中的每個像元計算坡度。對于不規則三角網 (TIN)，坡度為各三角形中的最大高程變化率。對于網格，坡度為每個像元及與其相鄰的八個像元中的最大高程變化率。
坡度命令可提取輸入表面網格，并計算出包含各個像元坡度的輸出網格。坡度值越小，地勢越平坦；坡度值越大，地勢越陡峭。可使用百分比單位計算輸出坡度網格，也可以以度為單位進行計算。
坡度角等于 45 度時，高程增量等于水平增量。如果以百分比形式表示，此角的坡度為 100%。如果坡度接近垂直（90 度），則百分比坡度接近無窮大。
坡度函數最常用于處理高程格網，如下圖所示。較為陡峭的坡在輸出坡度地圖中顯示為紅色。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/3d-analyst/understanding-slope.html

## 在兩個 TIN 間拉伸 (3D Analyst 工具) · 用法

**id**　01264abe9afcb0d152-2

**english**　Extrude Between

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 3D Analyst 工具箱
- 面積和體積工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/3d-analyst/extrude-between.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/3d-analyst/extrude-between.html

**text**　將僅在輸出中表示與兩個 TIN 的 XY 范圍重疊的輸入圖徵部分。
拉伸圖徵的幾何取決于輸入圖徵的幾何： 對于線和面輸入圖徵，將創建多面體幾何。 對于點輸入圖徵，將創建線幾何。
如果任何輸入資料集與另一個輸入資料集邊界重合，該工具可能無法生成有效的輸出。 如果輸入面與任意 TIN 表面的邊界重合，可以考慮使用 緩衝區 工具稍微修改面的形狀。 同樣，如果任一 TIN 表面在與面重疊的區域內與另一個表面的邊界重合，可以考慮通過交互式編輯或使用 TIN 范圍 工具修改 TIN 的資料區域，以將 TIN 的覆蓋區輸出為面，對面應用輕微緩衝區，并使用 編輯TIN 工具將面重新加載到 TIN 中。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/3d-analyst/extrude-between.html#usage
